2025-12-29 20:58:28.449 +05:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (1ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT "s"."Name", "s0"."Id", "s0"."Author", "s0"."AverageRating", "s0"."CategoryId", "s0"."CreatedAt", "s0"."Description", "s0"."ISBN", "s0"."ImageUrl", "s0"."ImgUrl", "s0"."IsDeleted", "s0"."IsFavorite", "s0"."IsFeatured", "s0"."LowStockThreshold", "s0"."Price", "s0"."StockQuantity", "s0"."Title", "s0"."UpdatedAt", "s0"."Id0"
FROM (
    SELECT "c0"."Name"
    FROM "Books" AS "b"
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT "c"."Id", "c"."Name"
        FROM "Categories" AS "c"
        WHERE NOT ("c"."IsDeleted")
    ) AS "c0" ON "b"."CategoryId" = "c0"."Id"
    WHERE NOT ("b"."IsDeleted")
    GROUP BY "c0"."Name"
) AS "s"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "b0"."Id", "b0"."Author", "b0"."AverageRating", "b0"."CategoryId", "b0"."CreatedAt", "b0"."Description", "b0"."ISBN", "b0"."ImageUrl", "b0"."ImgUrl", "b0"."IsDeleted", "b0"."IsFavorite", "b0"."IsFeatured", "b0"."LowStockThreshold", "b0"."Price", "b0"."StockQuantity", "b0"."Title", "b0"."UpdatedAt", "c1"."Id" AS "Id0", "c1"."Name"
    FROM "Books" AS "b0"
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT "c2"."Id", "c2"."Name"
        FROM "Categories" AS "c2"
        WHERE NOT ("c2"."IsDeleted")
    ) AS "c1" ON "b0"."CategoryId" = "c1"."Id"
    WHERE NOT ("b0"."IsDeleted")
) AS "s0" ON "s"."Name" = "s0"."Name"
ORDER BY "s"."Name", "s0"."Id"
